rule (modulo E) Register:
   [ Fr( ~skc ), Fr( ~skp ) ]
  --[ Reg( $A ), OnlyOnce( $A ), IdentityIs( $A, h(<pk(~skc), pk(~skp)>) )
  ]->
   [
   !LtkC( $A, ~skc ), !LtkP( $A, ~skp ), !Pk( $A, pk(~skc) ),
   !PkC( $A, pk(~skc) ), !PkP( $A, pk(~skp) ),
   !Kh( $A, h(<pk(~skc), pk(~skp)>) ), Out( <pk(~skc), pk(~skp)> )
   ]

  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

rule (modulo E) Compromise_Classical:
   [ !LtkC( $A, ~skc ) ]
  --[ Compromised( $A ), CompromisedClassical( $A ) ]->
   [ Out( ~skc ) ]

  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

rule (modulo E) Compromise_PostQuantum:
   [ !LtkP( $A, ~skp ) ]
  --[ Compromised( $A ), CompromisedPQ( $A ) ]->
   [ Out( ~skp ) ]

  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

rule (modulo E) Recognise_Without_Meeting:
   [ !LtkC( $V, ~vsc ), !LtkP( $V, ~vsp ), In( <$S, newkey, result> ) ]
  --[
  Recognised( $V, $S ), RecognisedFor( $V, $S, newkey ),
  RecognisedAs( $V, $S, newkey, result ), RecognisedWithoutMeeting( $V )
  ]->
   [
   Out( <'recognise', $S, newkey, result, 
         sign(<'rhtn/1:verifier', $S, newkey, result>, ~vsc), 
         sign(<'rhtn/1:verifier', $S, newkey, result>, ~vsp)>
   )
   ]

  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

rule (modulo E) Old_Key_Proof:
   [ !LtkC( $S, ~oldc ), !LtkP( $S, ~oldp ), In( newkey ), !Pk( $P, pkP ) ]
  --[ OldKeyProof( $S, newkey ), OldKeyProofFor( $S, newkey, $P ) ]->
   [
   Out( <'rotate', $S, newkey, $P, 
         sign(<'rhtn/1:successor', $S, newkey, $P>, ~oldc),

   )
   ]

  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

rule (modulo E) Met:
   [ ] --[ PriorCounterparty( $V, $S ) ]-> [ !Met( $V, $S ) ]

  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

rule (modulo E) Recovery_Meeting:
   [ !Met( $V, $S ), Fr( ~nk ) ]
  --[ RecoveryMeeting( $V, $S ), MeetingKey( $V, $S, ~nk ) ]->
   [ AtMeeting( $V, $S, ~nk ), Out( ~nk ) ]

  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

rule (modulo E) Recognise:
   [ !LtkC( $V, ~vsc ), !LtkP( $V, ~vsp ), AtMeeting( $V, $S, newkey ) ]
  --[
  Recognised( $V, $S ), RecognisedFor( $V, $S, newkey ),
  RecognisedAs( $V, $S, newkey, 'match' ),
  HonestlyRecognised( $V, $S, newkey )

restriction AtLeastOneMatchInTheBlock:
  "∀ r1 r2 #i.
    (BlockResults( r1, r2 ) @ #i) ⇒ ((r1 = 'match') ∨ (r2 = 'match'))"
  // safety formula

  /*
  expanded formula:
  "∀ r1 r2 #i.
    (BlockResults( r1, r2 ) @ #i) ⇒ ((r1 = 'match') ∨ (r2 = 'match'))"
  */

rule (modulo E) Transfer_Countersign:
   [
   !LtkC( $Old, ~oc ), !LtkP( $Old, ~op ), In( nodekey ), !Pk( $New, pkNew )
   ]
  --[ CountersignedFor( $Old, nodekey, $New ) ]->
   [
   Out( <'transfer', nodekey, $Old, $New, 
         sign(<'rhtn/1:transfer', nodekey, $Old, $New>, ~oc), 
         sign(<'rhtn/1:transfer', nodekey, $Old, $New>, ~op)>
   )
   ]

  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

rule (modulo E) Patron_Accept_Transfer:
   [
   !LtkC( $New, ~nsk ), !Kh( $Old, khOldT ), In( kmOldT ),
   In( <'transfer', nodekey, $Old, $New, provC, provP> )
   ]
  --[
  Eq( h(kmOldT), khOldT ),
  Eq( verify(provC, <'rhtn/1:transfer', nodekey, $Old, $New>, fst(kmOldT)),
      true
  ),
  Eq( verify(provP, <'rhtn/1:transfer', nodekey, $Old, $New>, snd(kmOldT)),
      true
  ),
  Distinct( $Old, $New ),
  PatronAcceptedTransferEvidence( $New, $Old, nodekey )
  ]->
   [ Out( sign(<'adopted', nodekey, $New>, ~nsk) ) ]

  /*
  rule (modulo AC) Patron_Accept_Transfer:
     [
     !LtkC( $New, ~nsk ), !Kh( $Old, khOldT ), In( kmOldT ),
     In( <'transfer', nodekey, $Old, $New, provC, provP> )
     ]
    --[
    Eq( h(kmOldT), khOldT ), Eq( z, true ), Eq( z.1, true ),
    Distinct( $Old, $New ),
    PatronAcceptedTransferEvidence( $New, $Old, nodekey )
    ]->
     [ Out( sign(<'adopted', nodekey, $New>, ~nsk) ) ]
    variants (modulo AC)
    1. $New  = $New.21

rule (modulo E) Patron_Accept_Recovery:
   [
   !LtkC( $P, ~psk ), !Kh( $S, khOld ), !Kh( $V, khV ), !Kh( $V2, khV2 ),
   In( <kmOld, kmV, kmV2> ),
   In( <'rotate', $S, newkey, $P, provAc, provAp> ),
   In( <'recognise', $S, newkey, result, provBc, provBp> ),
   In( <'recognise', $S, newkey2, result2, provB2c, provB2p> )
   ]
  --[
  Eq( h(kmOld), khOld ), Eq( h(kmV), khV ), Eq( h(kmV2), khV2 ),
  Eq( newkey2, newkey ), Distinct( $V, $V2 ),
  Eq( verify(provB2c, <'rhtn/1:verifier', $S, newkey2, result2>,
             fst(kmV2)),
      true
  ),
  Eq( verify(provB2p, <'rhtn/1:verifier', $S, newkey2, result2>,
             snd(kmV2)),
      true
  ),
  Eq( verify(provAc, <'rhtn/1:successor', $S, newkey, $P>, fst(kmOld)),
      true
  ),
  Eq( verify(provAp, <'rhtn/1:successor', $S, newkey, $P>, snd(kmOld)),
      true
  ),
  Eq( verify(provBc, <'rhtn/1:verifier', $S, newkey, result>, fst(kmV)),
      true
  ),
  Eq( verify(provBp, <'rhtn/1:verifier', $S, newkey, result>, snd(kmV)),
      true
  ),
  BlockResults( result, result2 ), Distinct( $V, $S ), Distinct( $P, $S ),
  Distinct( khOld, newkey ), ReliedOn( $P, $V, $S ),
  ReliedOn( $P, $V2, $S ), PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( $P, $S, newkey )
  ]->
   [ Out( sign(<'recovered', $S, newkey>, ~psk) ) ]

  /*

restriction OneKeyPerName:
  "∀ a #i #j. ((OnlyOnce( a ) @ #i) ∧ (OnlyOnce( a ) @ #j)) ⇒ (#i = #j)"
  // safety formula

  /*
  expanded formula:
  "∀ a #i #j. ((OnlyOnce( a ) @ #i) ∧ (OnlyOnce( a ) @ #j)) ⇒ (#i = #j)"
  */

restriction Equality:
  "∀ x y #i. (Eq( x, y ) @ #i) ⇒ (x = y)"
  // safety formula

  /*
  expanded formula:
  "∀ x y #i. (Eq( x, y ) @ #i) ⇒ (x = y)"
  */

restriction VerifierIsNotSubject:
  "∀ V S #i. (Distinct( V, S ) @ #i) ⇒ (¬(V = S))"
  // safety formula

  /*
  expanded formula:
  "∀ V S #i. (Distinct( V, S ) @ #i) ⇒ (¬(V = S))"
  */

lemma forging_a_recognition_needs_both_verifier_halves:
  all-traces
  "∀ P S newkey V #i.
    (((PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i) ∧
      (ReliedOn( P, V, S ) @ #i)) ∧
     (¬(∃ #k. CompromisedPQ( V ) @ #k))) ⇒
    (∃ #b. (RecognisedFor( V, S, newkey ) @ #b) ∧ (#b < #i))"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all counter-examples:
"∃ P S newkey V #i.
  (PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i) ∧
  (ReliedOn( P, V, S ) @ #i)
 ∧

lemma acceptance_requires_a_signed_match:
  all-traces
  "∀ P S newkey #i.
    (PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i) ⇒
    ((∃ V #b. (RecognisedAs( V, S, newkey, 'match' ) @ #b) ∧ (#b < #i)) ∨
     (∃ V #x #y.
       (((CompromisedClassical( V ) @ #x) ∧ (CompromisedPQ( V ) @ #y)) ∧
        (#x < #i)) ∧
       (#y < #i)))"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all counter-examples:
"∃ P S newkey #i.
  (PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i)
 ∧
  (∀ V #b. (RecognisedAs( V, S, newkey, 'match' ) @ #b) ⇒ ¬(#b < #i)) ∧
  (∀ V #x #y.
    (CompromisedClassical( V ) @ #x) ∧ (CompromisedPQ( V ) @ #y)
   ⇒
    ((¬(#x < #i)) ∨ (¬(#y < #i))))"
*/
simplify
solve( PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i )
  case Patron_Accept_Recovery
  solve( (result = 'match')  ∥ (result2 = 'match') )
    case case_1
    solve( !LtkC( $P, ~psk ) ▶₀ #i )
      case Register
      solve( !Kh( $S, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₁ #i )
        case Register
        solve( !Kh( $V, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₂ #i )
          case Register
          solve( !Kh( $V2, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₃ #i )
            case Register
            solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:successor', $S, newkey, $P>, ~skc) ) @ #vk.20 )
              case Old_Key_Proof
              solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:verifier', $S, newkey, 'match'>, ~skc.1)
                     ) @ #vk.26 )
                case Recognise
                by contradiction /* from formulas */
              next
                case Recognise_Without_Meeting
                by contradiction /* from formulas */
              next
                case c_sign
                solve( !KU( ~skc.1 ) @ #vk.42 )
                  case Compromise_Classical
                  solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:verifier', $S, newkey, 'match'>, ~skp.1)
                         ) @ #vk.31 )
                    case Recognise
                    by contradiction /* from formulas */
                  next
                    case Recognise_Without_Meeting
                    by contradiction /* from formulas */
                  next
                    case c_sign
                    solve( !KU( ~skp.1 ) @ #vk.44 )
                      case Compromise_PostQuantum
                      by contradiction /* from formulas */
                    qed
                  qed
                qed
              qed
            next
              case c_sign
              solve( !KU( ~skc ) @ #vk.42 )
                case Compromise_Classical
                solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:successor', $S, newkey, $P>, ~skp) ) @ #vk.25 )
                  case Old_Key_Proof
                  solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:verifier', $S, newkey, 'match'>, ~skc.1)
                         ) @ #vk.30 )
                    case Recognise
                    by contradiction /* from formulas */
                  next
                    case Recognise_Without_Meeting
                    by contradiction /* from formulas */
                  next
                    case c_sign
                    solve( !KU( ~skc.1 ) @ #vk.47 )
                      case Compromise_Classical
                      solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:verifier', $S, newkey, 'match'>, ~skp.1)
                             ) @ #vk.35 )
                        case Recognise
                        by contradiction /* from formulas */

lemma forging_a_recognition_needs_the_classical_half_too:
  all-traces
  "∀ P S newkey V #i.
    (((PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i) ∧
      (ReliedOn( P, V, S ) @ #i)) ∧
     (¬(∃ #k. CompromisedClassical( V ) @ #k))) ⇒
    (∃ #b. (RecognisedFor( V, S, newkey ) @ #b) ∧ (#b < #i))"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all counter-examples:
"∃ P S newkey V #i.
  (PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i) ∧
  (ReliedOn( P, V, S ) @ #i)
 ∧
  (∀ #k. (CompromisedClassical( V ) @ #k) ⇒ ⊥) ∧
  (∀ #b. (RecognisedFor( V, S, newkey ) @ #b) ⇒ ¬(#b < #i))"
*/
simplify
solve( PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i )
  case Patron_Accept_Recovery
  solve( (result = 'match')  ∥ (result2 = 'match') )
    case case_1
    solve( !LtkC( $P, ~psk ) ▶₀ #i )
      case Register
      solve( !Kh( $S, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₁ #i )
        case Register
        solve( !Kh( $V, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₂ #i )
          case Register
          solve( !Kh( $V2, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₃ #i )
            case Register
            solve( ReliedOn( $P, V.1, $S ) @ #i )
              case Patron_Accept_Recovery_case_1
              solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:successor', $S, newkey, $P>, ~skc) ) @ #vk.20 )

lemma a_recovery_never_installs_the_prior_identity:
  all-traces
  "∀ P S newkey #i #r.
    ((PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i) ∧
     (IdentityIs( S, newkey ) @ #r)) ⇒
    (⊥)"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all counter-examples:
"∃ P S newkey #i #r.
  (PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i) ∧
  (IdentityIs( S, newkey ) @ #r)"
*/
simplify
solve( PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, $A, h(<pk(~skc), pk(~skp)>)
       ) @ #i )
  case Patron_Accept_Recovery
  solve( (result = 'match')  ∥ (result2 = 'match') )
    case case_1
    solve( !LtkC( $P, ~psk ) ▶₀ #i )
      case Register
      solve( !Kh( $A, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₁ #i )
        case Register
        by contradiction /* from formulas */
      qed
    qed
  next
    case case_2
    solve( !LtkC( $P, ~psk ) ▶₀ #i )
      case Register
      solve( !Kh( $A, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₁ #i )
        case Register
        by contradiction /* from formulas */
      qed
    qed
  qed
next
  case Patron_Accept_Recovery_One_Response
  solve( !LtkC( $P, ~psk ) ▶₀ #i )
    case Register
    solve( !Kh( $A, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₁ #i )
      case Register
      by contradiction /* from formulas */
    qed
  qed
qed

lemma a_recognition_is_not_accepted_as_an_old_key_proof:
  all-traces
  "∀ P S newkey #i.
    (PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i) ⇒
    ((∃ #a. (OldKeyProofFor( S, newkey, P ) @ #a) ∧ (#a < #i)) ∨
     ((∃ #x. (CompromisedClassical( S ) @ #x) ∧ (#x < #i)) ∧
      (∃ #y. (CompromisedPQ( S ) @ #y) ∧ (#y < #i))))"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all counter-examples:
"∃ P S newkey #i.
  (PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i)
 ∧
  (∀ #a. (OldKeyProofFor( S, newkey, P ) @ #a) ⇒ ¬(#a < #i)) ∧
  (((∀ #x. (CompromisedClassical( S ) @ #x) ⇒ ¬(#x < #i)) ∨
    (∀ #y. (CompromisedPQ( S ) @ #y) ⇒ ¬(#y < #i))))"
*/
simplify
solve( (∀ #x. (CompromisedClassical( S ) @ #x) ⇒ ¬(#x < #i))  ∥
       (∀ #y. (CompromisedPQ( S ) @ #y) ⇒ ¬(#y < #i)) )
  case case_1
  solve( PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i )
    case Patron_Accept_Recovery
    solve( (result = 'match')  ∥ (result2 = 'match') )
      case case_1

        case Register
        solve( !Kh( $S, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₁ #i )
          case Register
          solve( !Kh( $V, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₂ #i )
            case Register
            solve( !Kh( $V2, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₃ #i )
              case Register
              solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:successor', $S, newkey, $P>, ~skc) ) @ #vk.20 )
                case Old_Key_Proof
                by contradiction /* from formulas */
              next
                case c_sign
                solve( !KU( ~skc ) @ #vk.42 )
                  case Compromise_Classical
                  by contradiction /* from formulas */
                qed
              qed
            qed
          qed
        qed
      qed
    next
      case case_2
      solve( !LtkC( $P, ~psk ) ▶₀ #i )
        case Register
        solve( !Kh( $S, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₁ #i )
          case Register
          solve( !Kh( $V, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₂ #i )
            case Register
            solve( !Kh( $V2, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₃ #i )
              case Register
              solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:successor', $S, newkey, $P>, ~skc) ) @ #vk.20 )

                by contradiction /* from formulas */

                  case Compromise_Classical
                  by contradiction /* from formulas */
                qed
              qed
            qed
          qed

    qed
  next
    case Patron_Accept_Recovery_One_Response

lemma recovery_completes_honestly:
  exists-trace
  "∃ P S newkey V #m #r #i.
    (((((((MeetingKey( V, S, newkey ) @ #m) ∧
          (HonestlyRecognised( V, S, newkey ) @ #r)) ∧
         (ReliedOn( P, V, S ) @ #i)) ∧
        (PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i)) ∧
       (#m < #r)) ∧
      (#r < #i)) ∧
     (¬(∃ A #k. Compromised( A ) @ #k))) ∧
    (¬(∃ X #w. RecognisedWithoutMeeting( X ) @ #w))"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all satisfying traces:
"∃ P S newkey V #m #r #i.
  (MeetingKey( V, S, newkey ) @ #m) ∧
  (HonestlyRecognised( V, S, newkey ) @ #r) ∧
  (ReliedOn( P, V, S ) @ #i) ∧
  (PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i)
 ∧
  (#m < #r) ∧
  (#r < #i) ∧
  (∀ A #k. (Compromised( A ) @ #k) ⇒ ⊥) ∧
  (∀ X #w. (RecognisedWithoutMeeting( X ) @ #w) ⇒ ⊥)"
*/
simplify
solve( !Met( $V, $S ) ▶₀ #m )
  case Met
  solve( !LtkC( $V, ~vsc ) ▶₀ #r )
    case Register
    solve( !LtkP( $V, ~vsp ) ▶₁ #r )
      case Register
      solve( AtMeeting( $V, $S, ~nk ) ▶₂ #r )
        case Recovery_Meeting
        solve( ReliedOn( P, $V, $S ) @ #i )
          case Patron_Accept_Recovery_One_Response
          solve( !LtkC( $P, ~psk ) ▶₀ #i )
            case Register
            solve( !Kh( $S, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₁ #i )
              case Register
              solve( !Kh( $V, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₂ #i )
                case Register
                solve( PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( $P, $S, ~nk ) @ #i )
                  case Patron_Accept_Recovery_One_Response
                  solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:successor', $S, ~nk, $P>, ~skc) ) @ #vk.15 )
                    case Old_Key_Proof
                    solve( !KU( ~nk ) @ #vk.11 )
                      case Recognise
                      solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:successor', $S, ~nk, $P>, ~skp) ) @ #vk.16 )
                        case Old_Key_Proof
                        solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:verifier', $S, ~nk, 'match'>, ~vsc)
                               ) @ #vk.21 )
                          case Recognise
                          solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:verifier', $S, ~nk, 'match'>, ~skp.1)
                                 ) @ #vk.22 )
                            case Recognise
                            solve( !KU( pk(~skc) ) @ #vk.23 )
                              case Register
                              solve( !KU( pk(~skp) ) @ #vk.24 )
                                case Register
                                solve( !KU( pk(~vsc) ) @ #vk.25 )
                                  case Register
                                  solve( !KU( pk(~skp.1) ) @ #vk.26 )
                                    case Register
                                    SOLVED // trace found
                                  qed
                                qed
                              qed
                            qed
                          qed
                        qed
                      qed
                    qed
                  qed
                qed
              qed
            qed
          qed
        qed
      qed
    qed
  qed
qed

lemma recovery_is_executable:
  exists-trace
  "∃ P S newkey #i. PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all satisfying traces:
"∃ P S newkey #i. (PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i)"
*/
simplify
solve( PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i )
  case Patron_Accept_Recovery_One_Response
  solve( !LtkC( $P, ~psk ) ▶₀ #i )
    case Register
    solve( !Kh( $S, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₁ #i )
      case Register
      solve( !Kh( $V, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₂ #i )
        case Register
        solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:successor', $S, newkey, $P>, ~skc) ) @ #vk.15 )
          case Old_Key_Proof
          solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:verifier', $S, newkey, 'match'>, ~skc.1)
                 ) @ #vk.21 )
            case Recognise
            solve( !KU( ~nk ) @ #vk.14 )
              case Recognise
              solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:successor', $S, ~nk, $P>, ~skp) ) @ #vk.19 )
                case Old_Key_Proof
                solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:verifier', $S, ~nk, 'match'>, ~skp.1)
                       ) @ #vk.22 )
                  case Recognise
                  solve( !KU( pk(~skc) ) @ #vk.23 )
                    case Register
                    solve( !KU( pk(~skp) ) @ #vk.24 )
                      case Register
                      solve( !KU( pk(~skc.1) ) @ #vk.25 )
                        case Register
                        solve( !KU( pk(~skp.1) ) @ #vk.26 )
                          case Register
                          SOLVED // trace found
                        qed
                      qed
                    qed
                  qed
                qed
              qed
            qed
          qed
        qed
      qed
    qed
  qed
qed

lemma recognition_alone_insufficient:
  all-traces
  "¬(∃ P S newkey #i.
      ((((PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i) ∧
         (∃ V #b. Recognised( V, S ) @ #b)) ∧
        (¬(∃ #a. OldKeyProof( S, newkey ) @ #a))) ∧
       (¬(∃ #c. Compromised( S ) @ #c))) ∧
      (¬(∃ #kp. Compromised( P ) @ #kp)))"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all counter-examples:
"∃ P S newkey #i.
  (PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i)
 ∧
  (∃ V #b. (Recognised( V, S ) @ #b)) ∧

*/

lemma recognition_binds_the_successor:
  all-traces
  "∀ P S newkey #i.
    (PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i) ⇒
    (((∃ V #b. (RecognisedFor( V, S, newkey ) @ #b) ∧ (#b < #i)) ∨
      (∃ V #kv.
        (((ReliedOn( P, V, S ) @ #i) ∧ (CompromisedClassical( V ) @ #kv)) ∧
         (#kv < #i)) ∧
        (∃ #ky. (CompromisedPQ( V ) @ #ky) ∧ (#ky < #i)))) ∨
     ((∃ #x. (CompromisedClassical( P ) @ #x) ∧ (#x < #i)) ∧
      (∃ #y. (CompromisedPQ( P ) @ #y) ∧ (#y < #i))))"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all counter-examples:
"∃ P S newkey #i.
  (PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i)
 ∧
  (∀ V #b. (RecognisedFor( V, S, newkey ) @ #b) ⇒ ¬(#b < #i)) ∧
  (∀ V #kv.
    (ReliedOn( P, V, S ) @ #i) ∧ (CompromisedClassical( V ) @ #kv)
   ⇒
    ((¬(#kv < #i)) ∨ (∀ #ky. (CompromisedPQ( V ) @ #ky) ⇒ ¬(#ky < #i)))) ∧
  (((∀ #x. (CompromisedClassical( P ) @ #x) ⇒ ¬(#x < #i)) ∨
    (∀ #y. (CompromisedPQ( P ) @ #y) ⇒ ¬(#y < #i))))"
*/
simplify
solve( (∀ #x. (CompromisedClassical( P ) @ #x) ⇒ ¬(#x < #i))  ∥
       (∀ #y. (CompromisedPQ( P ) @ #y) ⇒ ¬(#y < #i)) )
  case case_1
  solve( PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i )
    case Patron_Accept_Recovery
    solve( (result = 'match')  ∥ (result2 = 'match') )
      case case_1
      solve( !LtkC( $P, ~psk ) ▶₀ #i )

              case Register
              solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:successor', $S, newkey, $P>, ~skc) ) @ #vk.20 )
                case Old_Key_Proof
                solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:verifier', $S, newkey, 'match'>, ~skc.1)
                       ) @ #vk.26 )
                  case Recognise
                  by contradiction /* from formulas */
                next
                  case Recognise_Without_Meeting
                  by contradiction /* from formulas */
                next
                  case c_sign
                  solve( !KU( ~skc.1 ) @ #vk.42 )
                    case Compromise_Classical
                    solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:verifier', $S, newkey, 'match'>, ~skp.1)
                           ) @ #vk.31 )
                      case Recognise
                      by contradiction /* from formulas */
                    next
                      case Recognise_Without_Meeting
                      by contradiction /* from formulas */
                    next
                      case c_sign
                      solve( !KU( ~skp.1 ) @ #vk.44 )
                        case Compromise_PostQuantum
                        by contradiction /* from formulas */
                      qed
                    qed
                  qed
                qed

                solve( !KU( ~skc ) @ #vk.42 )

                           ) @ #vk.30 )

                          by contradiction /* from formulas */
                        next

                            by contradiction /* from formulas */

lemma successor_statement_binds_the_patron:
  all-traces
  "∀ P S newkey #i.
    (PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i) ⇒
    (((∃ #a. (OldKeyProofFor( S, newkey, P ) @ #a) ∧ (#a < #i)) ∨
      ((∃ #x. (CompromisedClassical( S ) @ #x) ∧ (#x < #i)) ∧
       (∃ #y. (CompromisedPQ( S ) @ #y) ∧ (#y < #i)))) ∨
     ((∃ #x. (CompromisedClassical( P ) @ #x) ∧ (#x < #i)) ∧
      (∃ #y. (CompromisedPQ( P ) @ #y) ∧ (#y < #i))))"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all counter-examples:
"∃ P S newkey #i.
  (PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i)
 ∧
  (∀ #a. (OldKeyProofFor( S, newkey, P ) @ #a) ⇒ ¬(#a < #i)) ∧
  (((∀ #x. (CompromisedClassical( S ) @ #x) ⇒ ¬(#x < #i)) ∨
    (∀ #y. (CompromisedPQ( S ) @ #y) ⇒ ¬(#y < #i)))) ∧
  (((∀ #x. (CompromisedClassical( P ) @ #x) ⇒ ¬(#x < #i)) ∨
    (∀ #y. (CompromisedPQ( P ) @ #y) ⇒ ¬(#y < #i))))"
*/
simplify
solve( (∀ #x. (CompromisedClassical( S ) @ #x) ⇒ ¬(#x < #i))  ∥
       (∀ #y. (CompromisedPQ( S ) @ #y) ⇒ ¬(#y < #i)) )
  case case_1
  solve( (∀ #x. (CompromisedClassical( P ) @ #x) ⇒ ¬(#x < #i))  ∥
         (∀ #y. (CompromisedPQ( P ) @ #y) ⇒ ¬(#y < #i)) )
    case case_1
    solve( PatronAcceptedRecoveryEvidence( P, S, newkey ) @ #i )
      case Patron_Accept_Recovery
      solve( (result = 'match')  ∥ (result2 = 'match') )
        case case_1
        solve( !LtkC( $P, ~psk ) ▶₀ #i )
          case Register
          solve( !Kh( $S, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₁ #i )
            case Register
            solve( !Kh( $V, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₂ #i )
              case Register
              solve( !Kh( $V2, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₃ #i )
                case Register
                solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:successor', $S, newkey, $P>, ~skc) ) @ #vk.20 )
                  case Old_Key_Proof
                  by contradiction /* from formulas */
                next
                  case c_sign
                  solve( !KU( ~skc ) @ #vk.42 )
                    case Compromise_Classical
                    by contradiction /* from formulas */
                  qed
                qed
              qed
            qed
          qed
        qed
      next
        case case_2
        solve( !LtkC( $P, ~psk ) ▶₀ #i )
          case Register
          solve( !Kh( $S, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₁ #i )
            case Register
            solve( !Kh( $V, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₂ #i )
              case Register
              solve( !Kh( $V2, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₃ #i )
                case Register
                solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:successor', $S, newkey, $P>, ~skc) ) @ #vk.20 )

  all-traces
  "∀ New Old nodekey #i.
    (PatronAcceptedTransferEvidence( New, Old, nodekey ) @ #i) ⇒
    ((∃ #a. (CountersignedFor( Old, nodekey, New ) @ #a) ∧ (#a < #i)) ∨
     ((∃ #x. (CompromisedClassical( Old ) @ #x) ∧ (#x < #i)) ∧

*/
